TRIGGER when: "페이지 구현해줘", "UI 구현해줘", "시안 작성해줘", "시안 적용해줘", 여러 컴포넌트가 연결된 복잡한 UI 구현, 설계(ux-designer) 결과를 받아 구현, 페이지 전체 레이아웃 구현 DO NOT TRIGGER when: 단일 컴포넌트 생성(component-builder 스킬 사용), UX 설계 필요(ux-designer 먼저), 설정 로직 구현(config-maker 사용), 단순 텍스트/카피 변경, 명확한 버그 수정 ([jsg3121/configDeck](https://github.com/jsg3121/configDeck/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ui-publisher.md) · ★1)

- **ui-reviewer**: Check DIAL design system, complete state coverage, accessibility/responsive behavior, no foreign donor appearance. ([Vanguduza/dial-new](https://github.com/Vanguduza/dial-new/blob/HEAD/dial-development-governor/agents/ui-reviewer.md) · 2 copies)

- **ui-role-surfaces**: How the assistant layer surfaces in the frontend, per role, per page. The shared model: every role sees the same components (ChatPanel global, ContextualAssistant on detail pages); what differs is the *capabilities matrix* — which skills are reachable, which quick actions are offered, and what the agent will or won't do. ([Razrocks/GitHub-Integrated-AI-Review-Platform](https://github.com/Razrocks/GitHub-Integrated-AI-Review-Platform/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/ui-role-surfaces.md))
- **ui-rows**: Use `DataRow` when repeated entity or progression records read across aligned fields. It is the shared pattern for that composition, whether the parent stacks rows or arranges compact rows in collection columns. Cards, tables, inventory tiles, singleton summaries and composite dashboard panels serve different tasks; labels or an internal grid alone do not make them data rows. ([Sludging/idleon-efficiency](https://github.com/Sludging/idleon-efficiency/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/ui-rows.md) · ★16)
- **ui-rules**: Two rules every UI change must respect. Full reference in [`docs/spec/architecture.md`](../spec/architecture.md) (DEC-007, DEC-008). ([energee/mgr](https://github.com/energee/mgr/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/ui-rules.md))
- **ui-rules-review**: Reviews UI code against this repo's locked look — the fits-the-screen rule (no horizontal scroll, mobile and desktop), the square-edge rule (no rounded corners), the Sato document identity and its ink rule, the no-ellipsis rule, desktop-tables/mobile-cards, and the exemptions that must NOT be brought into line (payroll exports, offer letters, the spotlight sheet, the premium closing workbook). Use before merging any change to a page, component, export or PDF. Read-only, and it hands back the exact browser measurement rather than guessing at it. ([sato-pharma/phoenix-people-workforce](https://github.com/sato-pharma/phoenix-people-workforce/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ui-rules-review.md))
- **ui-runner**: Drives on-device UI automation — taps, navigation, screenshots, view-hierarchy inspection — in an isolated context and reports back a verdict instead of the hierarchy. Use for any `mcp__argent__*` work. A single view hierarchy runs 30–55k tokens and is then re-read by every later turn of the session holding it, so this work does not belong in the orchestrator's context. You answer *what the UI did*; the `runner` answers *what a command did*; the `researcher` answers *why*. Does not fix code. ([chrfalch/ledger](https://github.com/chrfalch/ledger/blob/HEAD/agents/ui-runner.md) · ★1)
